# 1화 컷 이미지 프롬프트 — 정밀 셀 반실사 「소리의 색」

한 컷씩 붙여 넣는다. 참고 이미지(renders/characters·props·sets)를 함께 올리면 일관성이 오른다. 결과는 storyboard/renders/cel/<컷번호>.png로 저장한다. 말풍선·효과음은 이미지에 넣지 않는다.

## 1-001 — S1 갈대섬 작은 산 중턱 (새벽, 1987년 4월)
- 크기 800×1400 · ELS · 아이레벨 · 인물 없음
- 화면(한국어): 안개 낀 둑길을 고정으로 본다. 트럭은 보이지 않고 소리만 다가온다. 회청색 확산광이라 그림자가 거의 없다. 위쪽 1/3은 비워 타이틀을 얹는다.

```
[STYLE] clean uniform dark-brown 2px outlines, two-tone cel shading, semi-realistic proportions, soft gradient only in sky and backlight, no paper texture, no film grain, vertical webtoon panel, panel 800x1400 (tall)
[SCENE] on the small flat-topped hill of trash: terraced slope of compacted ochre soil mixed with scrap metal, broken boards, white plastic sheets and tin cans, a dump-truck ramp cut into the slope, the Han River and the 1980s city skyline far behind, 1987 early April, before dawn, thick fog, the big flat-topped hill and the embankment road below half-hidden in mist, cool blue-gray diffuse light with almost no shadows, at the end the first sunlight breaking over the big hill from the left (east), key color #5E7F99, cel shadows #34465E
[SET] the narrow earthen embankment road leading into the island, reeds on both sides, a cluster of shacks at the island entrance. Seen from the bare earth yard at the island end, looking south across the yard to the start of the road and on toward the mainland: on the near left a plywood notice board on two posts at the road's left shoulder; from there the narrow dirt road, about 5 m wide, runs straight away on top of a raised earth embankment about 3 m high, its surface rutted by truck tires with muddy water in the ruts; grassy earth banks slope down on both sides into tall reeds and shallow water; far away at the end of the road the low houses of the mainland and a hazy 1980s city skyline. Camera position: fixed from the island end of the road looking south toward the mainland. (keep the layout consistent with the reference image renders/sets/dyke_road.png)
[CUT] extreme long shot, static camera, eye level: the empty embankment road running straight into thick dawn fog, no vehicle visible yet, reeds on both sides fading into gray, flat blue-gray diffused pre-dawn light with almost no shadows, quiet composition with the upper third of the panel left as empty misty sky for the title
[NEG] no text, no letters, no speech bubbles, no brand logos, no real banknotes, no official Olympic mascot or emblem, fictional characters not resembling any real person, children drawn as ordinary kids, no injuries or blood
```

**레터링(후공정, 이미지에 넣지 않음)**
  - [caption] 제목: 1화 귀신과 깡통
  - [caption] 자막: 1987년 4월
  - [sfx] SFX(작게): 부르릉

## 1-002 — S1 갈대섬 작은 산 중턱 (새벽, 1987년 4월)
- 크기 800×1000 · MS · 아이레벨 · 은주(좌), 동민(우), 갈고리 아주머니(우·후경)
- 화면(한국어): 은주가 쪼그려 앉아 목장갑을 고쳐 낀다. 동민은 빈 자루를 끌어안고 꾸벅꾸벅 존다. 뒤 비탈에서 갈고리를 쥔 아주머니가 은주 쪽을 보며 소리친다(말풍선 우상단).

```
[STYLE] clean uniform dark-brown 2px outlines, two-tone cel shading, semi-realistic proportions, soft gradient only in sky and backlight, no paper texture, no film grain, vertical webtoon panel, panel 800x1000 (vertical)
[SCENE] on the small flat-topped hill of trash: terraced slope of compacted ochre soil mixed with scrap metal, broken boards, white plastic sheets and tin cans, a dump-truck ramp cut into the slope, the Han River and the 1980s city skyline far behind, 1987 early April, before dawn, thick fog, the big flat-topped hill and the embankment road below half-hidden in mist, cool blue-gray diffuse light with almost no shadows, at the end the first sunlight breaking over the big hill from the left (east), key color #5E7F99, cel shadows #34465E
[SET] Seen from halfway up the south slope, on one of its wide terraces, looking southeast and down: the terraces step down like giant stairs of packed ochre and gray-brown trash; below, the low shack roofs of the village at the island entrance and the narrow embankment road running south through the reeds; on the left, at the foot of the bigger flat-topped hill, a dump truck tipping its load onto a heap; the ramp road climbing the slope beside the terraces. (keep the layout consistent with the reference image renders/sets/small_hill_slope.png)
[CHAR] Eunju, 13-year-old Korean girl, 140cm, small and thin, sun-tanned skin, narrow small face, large long almond-shaped eyes, thick straight eyebrows, no bangs with the forehead visible, loose wisps of hair falling at both temples, faint freckles on nose and cheeks, long dark-brown hair tied low at the nape with a single rubber band, tilts her head slightly to the left when listening, a small silver tuning-fork pendant on a thin chain; outfit in this scene: an oversized navy work jacket (hand-me-down), worn trousers with frayed knees, plain white knitted cotton work gloves with no rubber coating, rubber boots
[CHAR] Dongmin, 9-year-old Korean boy, 125cm, round face, very short uneven clipper buzz cut (same length all over, no fade), upper-left front tooth missing, big ears, adhesive bandage on the right knee; outfit in this scene: an oversized hand-me-down loose green cotton-knit tracksuit with a single contrasting stripe down the sides, baggy at the knees, elastic cuffs, no logo, plain white knitted cotton work gloves with no rubber coating, rubber boots
[CHAR] a middle-aged Korean woman scavenger with a head scarf, dark padded work clothes, a large bamboo basket strapped on her back, holding a long iron hook
[PROP] a woven plastic scrap sack, a rectangular sack of woven white plastic strips, the weave texture visible, shown empty and folded and full and lumpy with scrap metal, mouth tied with string, woven polypropylene, 60 by 90cm, stained yellow-brown with mud, darker at the bottom, one or two frayed holes (match the reference image renders/props/scrap_sack.png)
[PROP] a long iron scavenging hook, a straight iron rod curved into a hook at the end with a wooden handle, iron, wood, 90cm long, rusty, handle polished by hands (match the reference image renders/props/iron_hook.png)
[CUT] medium shot, eye level: Eunju crouches on the left adjusting her cotton gloves, wiggling her toes inside her boots; Dongmin sits on the right hugging an empty sack, nodding off with his eyes shut; behind them on the slope the hook woman stands gripping her long iron hook, calling toward Eunju with her mouth open; flat blue-gray pre-dawn light, almost no shadows, empty space at upper right for a speech bubble
[NEG] no text, no letters, no speech bubbles, no brand logos, no real banknotes, no official Olympic mascot or emblem, fictional characters not resembling any real person, children drawn as ordinary kids, no injuries or blood
```

  - [dialogue] 갈고리 아주머니: 귀신아, 저건 어디 차냐?

- 화면(한국어): 은주 시점으로 더미를 천천히 훑는 구도. 은주의 어깨 너머로 쇳조각과 진흙 덩어리가 펼쳐진다. 은주가 담담하게 내기를 걸고, 미자는 받자마자 목장갑 낀 손으로 더미를 헤집어 노랗게 반짝이는 쇳덩이를 번쩍 들어 올린다. 뒤에서 패거리가 와아 소리친다.

```
[STYLE] clean uniform dark-brown 2px outlines, two-tone cel shading, semi-realistic proportions, soft gradient only in sky and backlight, no paper texture, no film grain, vertical webtoon panel, panel 800x1200 (tall)
[SCENE] at the foot of the big trash hill where dump trucks unload: a fresh heap of scrap iron and tin, tire ruts in the mud, 1987 early April, morning, clear but dusty hazy beige sky, low morning sun from the left (east), short metallic glints on the scrap heap, key color #C9A66B, cel shadows #6E5640
[SET] Seen from the mud at the foot of the heap, looking up and south: a heap about 4 m high of freshly dumped scrap iron, tin cans, bent pipes and sheet metal at the end of the truck road at the southwest foot of the big hill; deep tire ruts in the mud around it; mud-covered lumps scattered at its base; beyond the top of the heap the hazy beige sky, and on the right in the distance the smaller flat-topped trash hill. Camera position: slow pan across the heap at eye level. (keep the layout consistent with the reference image renders/sets/scrap_heap.png)
[CHAR] Eunju, 13-year-old Korean girl, 140cm, small and thin, sun-tanned skin, narrow small face, large long almond-shaped eyes, thick straight eyebrows, no bangs with the forehead visible, loose wisps of hair falling at both temples, faint freckles on nose and cheeks, long dark-brown hair tied low at the nape with a single rubber band, tilts her head slightly to the left when listening, a small silver tuning-fork pendant on a thin chain; outfit in this scene: an oversized navy work jacket (hand-me-down), worn trousers with frayed knees, plain white knitted cotton work gloves with no rubber coating, rubber boots
[CHAR] Mija, 14-year-old Korean girl, 158cm, tall for her age, broad shoulders, choppy self-cut short bob with bangs sticking out in all directions, thick eyebrows, square jaw, an adhesive bandage across the nose; outfit in this scene: a loose-fitting red cotton-knit tracksuit top with a single contrasting stripe down each sleeve, zip front, no logo, dark trousers, plain white knitted cotton work gloves with no rubber coating, rubber boots
[PROP] a lump of iron painted yellow, an irregular metal lump with yellow paint, one side scraped to dark iron, iron, paint, 20 by 12 by 8cm, scratched paint (match the reference image renders/props/yellow_painted_iron.png)
[CUT] medium shot over Eunju's shoulder: the view sweeps across the scrap heap, a jumble of rusty iron, tin and mud-covered lumps; Mija on the upper right digs with her gloved hands and triumphantly holds up a shiny yellow lump of metal, mouth wide in a boast; two gloved kids behind her throw up their arms; low morning sun from the left making the yellow paint gleam
[NEG] no text, no letters, no speech bubbles, no brand logos, no real banknotes, no official Olympic mascot or emblem, fictional characters not resembling any real person, children drawn as ordinary kids, no injuries or blood
```

**레터링(후공정, 이미지에 넣지 않음)**
  - [dialogue] 은주: 그럼 내기해요. 구리 먼저 찾는 사람이 여기 가져요.
  - [dialogue] 미자: 좋아. 지면 딴 데로 가.
  - [sfx] SFX: 철컥, 덜그럭
  - [dialogue] 미자: 봐라! 금빛이다!
  - [sfx] SFX: 와아

## 1-028 — S3 곽 영감 고물상 (오후)
- 크기 800×1400 · INS · POV(은주 시점) · 곽 영감(손·아래)
- 화면(한국어): 가로 2분할. 위: 은주 시선을 따라 선반으로 다가간다. 먼지가 뽀얀 고물 사이, 허리가 잘록한 나무 몸통 하나. 목은 부러져 없고 앞판에 금이 한 줄, 줄도 없다. 그것만 먼지 없이 측광을 받는다. 아래: 영감의 손 클로즈업. 고물을 집던 빠른 손이 느려져 갓난아이를 받듯 몸통을 천천히 내린다. 부드러운 천으로 앞판의 금을 따라 먼지를 닦고, 굽은 약지와 새끼손가락이 나무 위에서 조심스럽다.

```
[STYLE] clean uniform dark-brown 2px outlines, two-tone cel shading, semi-realistic proportions, soft gradient only in sky and backlight, no paper texture, no film grain, vertical webtoon panel, panel 800x1400 (tall)
[SCENE] old Kwak's junk shop at the island entrance: a corrugated-iron shed, sorted piles of scrap copper, brass and aluminum, a platform scale with a round dial, wooden apple crates by the door, 1987 early April, afternoon, windy, shaded interior under the corrugated roof, side light coming through the open door from the embankment road, the one wooden violin body on the shelf catching clean light, key color #8A6A4A, cel shadows #4E3B2C
[SET] Camera position: over the workbench toward the shelf on the east wall. (keep the layout consistent with the reference image renders/sets/junk_shop_in.png)
[PROP] the body of a broken violin with its neck snapped off, only the narrow-waisted violin body with two f-holes, splintered wood where the neck used to be, no strings, no bridge, a single crack running along the top, spruce and maple, most varnish worn off, 35.5cm long, 20cm wide, neck missing, one 12cm crack in the top, but wiped completely free of dust (match the reference image renders/props/broken_violin_body__s1.png)
[PROP] a soft polishing cloth, a small square of soft flannel, cotton flannel, 30 by 30cm, pilled from washing (match the reference image renders/props/polishing_cloth.png)
[CUT] tall panel split horizontally into two frames. Top: point-of-view shot from Eunju, slow push-in to a shelf where a wooden violin body with a narrow waist sits, neck broken off, one crack in the top, no strings, the only dustless thing among dusty scrap, catching the side light. ```
```

## 1-033 — S3 곽 영감 고물상 (오후)

- 화면(한국어): 가로 2분할. 위(와이드): 해가 강 너머로 기울어 쓰레기 산 전체가 금빛이다(화면 오른쪽 낮은 해, 긴 그림자). 꼭대기에서 목장갑 낀 은주가 고물을 고른다. 근처에 미자네 패거리, 사탕을 빠는 동민, 누룽지를 쥔 덕수. 순례 할머니의 검정 장화를 신은 선영이 숨을 헐떡이며 막 올라선다. 아래: 비탈 아래에서 위로. 천으로 싼 길쭉한 꾸러미를 안은 곽 영감이 느리고 무거운 걸음으로 올라온다. 영감의 등 너머 꼭대기 가장자리에서 아이들 손이 하나둘 멈추고, 은주가 고개를 왼쪽으로 기울인다.

## 1-073 — S11 작은 산 꼭대기 (저녁, 나흘째)

## 1-074 — S11 작은 산 꼭대기 (저녁, 나흘째)
- 크기 800×1600 · MS · 아이레벨 · 곽 영감(우), 선영(중), 은주(좌)
- 화면(한국어): 가로 2분할. 위: 영감이 짧게 말하고 깡통을 선영에게 건넨다. 선영이 받아 들고 턱에 대려다 멈춘다. 활을 쥔 선영의 손끝과 활 끝이 가늘게 떨린다. 안경을 고쳐 쓰고 애써 웃으며 사양하자, 영감이 깡통을 받아 은주에게 내민다. 아래: 두 손 극접사. 목장갑을 벗어 점퍼 주머니에 넣은 은주가 활을 쇠막대 쥐듯 손바닥 전체로 움켜쥐자, 영감의 굽은 약지·새끼손가락의 손이 은주의 손가락을 하나씩 펴 준다. 검지는 활 위, 엄지는 그 아래. 나무 숟가락 목, 구부린 포크 줄받침, 낡은 활이 함께 보인다.

```

[STYLE] clean uniform dark-brown 2px outlines, two-tone cel shading, semi-realistic proportions, soft gradient only in sky and backlight, no paper texture, no film grain, vertical webtoon panel, panel 800x1000 (vertical)
```
```
[SCENE] old Kwak's junk shop at the island entrance: a corrugated-iron shed, sorted piles of scrap copper, brass and aluminum, a platform scale with a round dial, wooden apple crates by the door, 1987 late April, afternoon over several days, shaded interior with side light from the open door, backlit X-ray film held up toward the sun; exterior wide: hazy beige sky over the junk-shop roof, crows rising from the big hill, key color #E9853A, cel shadows #6E5640

[CHAR] Mija, 14-year-old Korean girl, 158cm, tall for her age, broad shoulders, choppy self-cut short bob with bangs sticking out in all directions, thick eyebrows, square jaw, an adhesive bandage across the nose; outfit in this scene: a loose-fitting red cotton-knit tracksuit top with a single contrasting stripe down each sleeve, zip front, no logo, dark trousers
```

```
[PROP] 'Kkwaekkwaegi', a homemade trumpet made from a bent drainpipe, a 3cm-wide galvanized iron drainpipe bent into a flattened S with two bends, the mouth end wrapped three times with black electrical tape, a tin funnel jammed onto the other end as the bell, the joint between pipe and funnel bound with iron wire, an extra turn of wire and a small black rubber patch on the left side of the joint, galvanized iron pipe, tinplate funnel, iron wire, black rubber, black tape, about 55cm long and 18cm wide as bent, funnel bell 16cm across, gray zinc coating flaking off in places with brown rust spots, one dent in the funnel (match the reference image renders/props/kkwaekkwaegi__s2_fixed.png)
```

- 화면(한국어): 가로 3분할. 위: 압정 하나가 손가락 사이로 빠져 진흙에 떨어진다(극접사). 손이 잠깐 망설이다 집어 올려 흰 손수건으로 닦는다. 가운데: 마지막 귀퉁이를 누른 손. 같은 손수건이 프레임 위쪽으로 올라가 이마의 땀을 닦는다(얼굴은 프레임 밖). 둑길 갈대에 아침 이슬. 아래: 게시판 고정. 회색 소매가 둑길을 건너 작게 멀어진다. 소리만 멀어진다.

```
[STYLE] clean uniform dark-brown 2px outlines, two-tone cel shading, semi-realistic proportions, soft gradient only in sky and backlight, no paper texture, no film grain, vertical webtoon panel, panel 800x1800 (tall)
[SCENE] the narrow earthen embankment road leading into the island, reeds on both sides, a cluster of shacks at the island entrance; a weathered plywood notice board on two posts at the island entrance, pinned papers, 1987 early May, cool early morning, dew on the reeds, low clear sunlight from the left (east), key color #8C8F94, cel shadows #4A5060
[SET] Camera position: from the yard facing the board (south). (keep the layout consistent with the reference image renders/sets/notice_board.png)
[PROP] brass thumbtacks, flat round brass heads with short pins, shown loose and in a small tin box, brass, steel, head 1cm across, new, one tack smeared with mud (match the reference image renders/props/thumbtacks.png)
[PROP] a plain white cotton handkerchief, a square handkerchief with a narrow stitched hem, shown flat, folded in four and crumpled in a hand, thin white cotton, 40 by 40cm, clean and ironed (match the reference image renders/props/choi_handkerchief__s1_dry.png)
[PROP] a gray off-the-rack business suit, a gray single-breasted suit with white shirt and a patterned tie, wool blend, middle-aged man's size, sweat marks under the arms, baggy knees (match the reference image renders/props/choi_gray_suit.png)
[PROP] a weathered plywood notice board on two posts, a plywood panel between two wooden posts with a narrow little roof board on top, old pin holes and scraps of torn paper, plywood, wood (match the reference image renders/props/notice_board.png)
[PROP] a gray official notice sheet with a red seal, a portrait sheet of thin gray paper written horizontally: a blank title line and rows of illegible horizontal typed text marks (match the reference image renders/props/clearance_notice_1987__s1.png)
[PROP] a contest notice poster with a gold border, the poster pinned flat with four thumbtacks at its corners, a bold blank title bar and illegible text marks inside a double gold border (match the reference image renders/props/contest_poster__s2_pinned.png)
[CUT] tall panel split horizontally into three frames, the man's face never in frame. Top: extreme close-up, a thumbtack slips from his fingers into the mud, then the hand picks it up and wipes it with a white handkerchief. Middle: the hand presses the last corner, then the same handkerchief rises out of the top of the frame toward the forehead. Bottom: static shot of the board as a small gray-suited back walks away down the road
[NEG] no text, no letters, no speech bubbles, no brand logos, no real banknotes, no official Olympic mascot or emblem, fictional characters not resembling any real person, children drawn as ordinary kids, no injuries or blood
```

## 1-108 — S17 섬 어귀 게시판 (아침, 조금 뒤)
- 크기 800×1400 · LS · 아이레벨 · 은주(중)
